a call that isn't a listed part paid from an empty box; a tester's Charge during a hold added to what is owed and paid when the developer pays; a piece's cap, the part's max in all: an unlisted written piece at $1,000 in all, a $20 written listing paid up to $1,000 in all ("up to $990.00 more ($1,000.00 in all)" after a $10 Pay), a listing at the max with no Pay more room, a 30-minute call (listed or not) at $100 in all, five hours of unlisted calls reaching the $1,000 total, a dispute's range ending at the cap; "Paid the most this piece can be paid ($X)." at the cap for the developer, and the tester's three lines on a paid part (below the cap, at it, after Pay more closed); paying $0 on a listed and an unlisted part (the reason required, the row closed, the dispute still open); the note on Pay and Pay more (required at $0 and below the listing, 1,000 characters, never in the bell); the room beside the amount turning red as you type; the call's Rate and Amount filling each other (the last typed wins); the amount tick unticking when the amount changes, and the second tick over $200; a payment-service error that isn't a decline (no hold, nothing owed); a payment that never answered, checked after 15 minutes both ways (it went through, it didn't); a press repeated after a lost answer never acting twice; close-out (the date with late joiners, a pause moving it, a piece whose Charge opened late keeping 14 days, a suspended tester's extension, Pay, Pay more, Charge and Rate refused after it, the "closes on" lines in the last 7 days, the closing-soon countdown (a piece closing in 40 days getting all four marks, one closing in 5 days getting 3 days and 1 day, two tests in one line) and the tester's one daily "Charge or let go" line, with email Off, a piece closing unpaid on both sides, a dispute decided after close-out); the bank's check inline (Pay and Pay more confirmed and cancelled, Pay what you owe and Verify a card with a check, the button locked while it's open, an away charge confirmed from the test page through the Payments link and the dashboard's box (the bell's one line opening that box), the stand-in in recordings); money boxes rounding to the cent (1.4545 → $1.45 when the box is left and on the server, 0.495 → $0.50); a tester's Charge with no tick, and over $200 with its required tick; a chargeback hold (Pay what you owe disabled and refused before the card is verified; Pay what you owe reserving, "Uncaptured" in Stripe, with the bank's check inline when asked; Approve taking it once, paying the testers and lifting the hold; a Charge after reserving: Pay what you owe disabled with "… $Y added since will be charged right after.", the admin's "$X reserved · $Y added since …", Approve going through, the added amount charged once right after the lift (receipt naming the Charge), the bank asking (the confirm box) and a decline (a payment hold); Don't approve releasing it with the hold staying; a reservation expiring after about 7 days, with the developer's line and "Reservation expired" for the admin; a won chargeback releasing what was reserved); Confirm with your bank stopping once like Pay (a tester's dispute, a stopped test or a Pay in another tab, then the second press opening the check; already confirmed in another tab, expired and cancelled refused plainly); a Pay more stopping each tester press once with Alerts On (Cancel call included) and going straight through with Off; Dispute open below the piece's cap and disabled at it (a listed piece paid above its listing but below its cap disputed and raised to the cap, an unlisted piece at its max, a call at the $1,000, one cent below still allowed, a Pay more reaching it live, an old page refused, the server refusing a decision above the cap; the admin's "Their chat on this test" fold); payment lines emailed the same evening with email Off, Daily, Weekly and Monthly, and nothing else with Off; Verify a card's form on real Stripe test mode (the form drawn, Verify this card enabled once it's ready, the bank's check inline); Subscribe on real Stripe test mode with and without the bank's check, and a cancelled check cancelling the subscription; a share that waited for payout setup sent from its charge with no platform balance; number boxes ("-20", "0", "1e3" and "2.5" typed or pasted into spots, a minus in a Pay amount, the spots arrows at 1).
- **Participation:** two testers for the last spot; joining on the first day, the 13th day and the last day of a 14-day test (each gets 14 days); joining refused after Open until, and applications and invites closing then; a test past Open until no longer counting as open; Open until moved later by some days while testers work (every working deadline moves the same days); invite-only tests never in Find, and a test that became invite only staying on an applicant's dashboard; Open until under 3 or over 30 days refused; an 11th open test refused, and a post that goes through after another tab stopped a test; leaving during a suspension; pausing mid-send; closing mid-review; an invite accepted in one tab after it was cancelled in another; invite plus rejoin ask in either order; the 10-test limit on every way in; each tester's own deadline reminders (joining with 40, 10 and 2 days to go, a deadline moved later, a paused test); Open until on Edit capped at 30 days after the test opened (extended within the cap, refused past it at the field and on the server, a pause shift past it, a Next round starting fresh, the Edit hint's numbers); Find's "Most time to finish" order; a block either way hiding the developer's other tests (Find, a direct link, bell lines, Ask to rejoin) and closing waiting applications, and unblocking bringing them back; tester invitations closing for each reason (the card gone live, the refused press with its line), sent again on a new listing, and on a full test; Search by name finding a tester older than the newest 500, never a blocked or deleted one; Search by developer on the tester dashboard (the name-search rule from 1 character, an old ended test, the no-match line); Find tests' search with the chips and filters, and the no-match line; admins' Find tests (every state, the state ticks, drafts, no Join or Apply); Tests visited (each state, invite only and not invited, Remove, a visit then joining, a blocked developer, a state changing live); a developer profile's Open tests (each change leaving the list live, a stale click opening only the reason); No thanks on a full test, a closed invite and a re-sent invite; updated Terms of Use (an older version on Post, posting a draft, Join, Apply, Accept and Ask to rejoin: the tick above the button, required, red when unticked; ticked going through; the current version showing no tick; other presses unaffected); a developer profile's Open tests for a tester who is part of a test (working in a full invite-only test, invited to a full test, a pending application); Invite disabled while the test is full, paused or joining closed (the line, ticks kept, enabled live when a spot opens, an old page's press refused); Invite stops only for required devices and languages (an unrelated one, one added, removed, both, added then removed, several edits in one line); Invite anyway and Join anyway naming both kinds (long lists cut to 3, five testers in the scroll box); name searches from 1 character in Invite, Find, Search by developer and admin Accounts (a one-letter name, "an" and "ann", the exact name first); blocked profiles both ways (Unblock and "Unblocked.", the hidden Open tests line, "This profile isn't available.", "This page doesn't exist."); "Worked with you on N tests" with each state.
- **Terms:** an offer replaced by a newer one before the tester answers; the Switch / Keep line when questions changed (with and without answers sent); work sent during a change; reviewing at an old price; rejoining with the right terms; the per-field 3-change limit on 7 fields (one Save changing two fields), counted from the first join, application or invite and still counting after the only applicant withdrew, and Allow 3 more; applications closed and invites re-sent, stacked across tests; a Switch shown as one quiet line and a Keep not at all; questions changed after a draft (a draft answer to Q1 kept, a changed Q2 empty, Q3 unchanged, and the previous set showing all three); an open offer blocking each Send and call press, a second press still refused, an old page refused by the server and moved to the offer, Keep and Switch both unlocking, and Charge, Dispute and Leave not blocked.
- **Calls:** overlapping bookings; cancelling from another tab, then a stale Join; a hold or suspension starting within 3 days of a call, and one starting later; joining a paused test's call; requesting a call on a test with no Live call part; booking while terms are edited; a Forever set adding its next call; nobody joining by halfway; the measured length (a reload gap under 5 minutes, the 150% cap, no overlap at all); a listed call's automatic charge 7 days after it (charged once at the measured length × rate even with the run going twice, nothing when it was paid before, the bank asking, a decline starting a hold, added to what is owed during a hold, charged on reinstatement after a suspension, waiting on a team pause, 0 minutes closing with nothing to pay, an unlisted call untouched, the 3-day and 1-day reminders, the Pay box's and the tester's lines, no Charge or Let it go anywhere and an old page's press refused); the $1,000 for one tester's calls (a mix of listed and unlisted calls, booking refused at the limit, the warning under an hour's worth, an automatic charge cut by it); the weekly "Pay your calls" line stacking across tests; calls that are over moving to the tester row and Call history; the Call requests panel (a request arriving live while the form is open, a Needs you row opening it with the request highlighted); clash checks while filling Request a time (the same time as the other side's request booking at once, an overlap with each kind of call or request, two rows in one form, a weekly set with a later clash); a call reaching the $1,000 mid-way (the warning line, the ended line on both sides, Join refused after, the length capped, an unlisted call at $200/h); the Length capped by what's left (the hint, red at the box, refused on the server, the smallest of several ticked testers named, Accept of a longer request); the call room (only the booked tester and the developer get in, "This call isn't available." for anyone else, before 10 minutes ahead and after the end, checked on every request; camera, microphone, Mute, Camera off, Share screen with and without a camera; the status lines; Leave, Rejoin, and a fresh connection when either comes back); measuring from the connection (only the time both browsers were connected, a gap under 5 minutes counting, a longer one not, being in the room unconnected counting nothing); each server-side end (150%, the $1,000, a block either way, a pause for each cause, a suspension of either side, a leave, End early, a Stop) hanging up both rooms with its line, the minutes kept and no rejoin, calls ahead cancelled; the "This call ends at <time>." warning 2 minutes before 150% or the $1,000, in each reader's own time; a strict network (the line, nothing measured, cancelled with no charge once both left, and connecting with TURN set); the 15-minute reminder (the same line on both sides, none for a call booked less than 15 minutes ahead); Check your call setup (the camera preview and microphone level, a refused permission, never making a call or reaching the server); no red "overlaps" line at any moment after a Request a time press goes through (both sides, Once, Daily and Weekly, right after the answer and after the next poll); the past-calls scroll boxes (2 and 6 calls, a bell link to the 5th); the typed request kept across a reload.
- **Ratings:** complete and rate; the tester's Rate disabled with each of its lines (work to charge or let go, an open dispute, a review window with its date) and updating live; the developer's Rate hidden until the tester rated and everything is settled, then its 14 days and the "finished" bell line; a dispute opened after the tester rated disabling the developer's Rate until decided, and its decision stopping it once; the developer not rating in 14 days; a never-rated tester rated after close-out (only when everything was settled); the blind reveal (both showing together, the tester's alone after the window); no rating during your own suspension or a developer's hold (Rate hidden, an old page refused, the 14 days and reminders pausing); the other side suspended or held (straight through, no notice); Rate on a team-paused test for both sides; each change that stops Rate once for each side, and an ignored change going straight through; Ratings to give on both dashboards (rows appearing and going live, a stale click refused); leaving early; ended by the developer; an unpaid unlisted call no longer blocking rating after its review date; leave and rejoin without duplicate records; a tester rating while the developer's page is open (an ended tester: the live line with the window, the Rate box and Needs you row on the next poll, no stop; a working tester: one "finished their part (rated you)" stop under Testers leaving); Rate stopping once for a call change between the two, never for another tester's call.
- **Restrictions:** a developer pressing on a test page as a hold starts (routed to the dashboard top, blue box naming the press); the same on Profile and Edit; Cancel call from the Calls box during a hold; suspended on the session or payout page; a deleted user on an open page; two admins on one account; a lifted suspension and Ask to rejoin; long pauses closing; repeated pauses; a tester sending, saving a draft, charging or chatting while a test is paused, and the paused banner listing only what's available (with and without the deadline line); deadlines and Open until moving later when a pause lifts; a 6th Contact us message; a restricted account opening each closed page, a bell line and Change card; deleting with work still waiting; a mixed account with the developer side suspended (the tester side still joins, sends work and chats; the side switch lands on the blue box); the paused view during a team pause and during a hold (Stop, End early and Cancel call stopping once for news since the page loaded, an older page's Save refused and routed); chat while the other person is restricted (read-only note, a Send turned back with the text kept, the box coming back live); the developer's side of a dispute from the dashboard box while suspended; every allowed press keeping the usual stop-once when a test is stopped, closed or paused, a tester's work ended, or a developer held or suspended (on a stopped test a tester's dispute stopping the developer's Pay once; on a paused test a payment elsewhere stopping Charge once), with Rate keeping its own list and excerpts, "Send your side", Leave and Block never stopping; a stop or a pause while a page is open (each press type with each kind: the developer's Stop, the team's, a close, End early, the team's pause, a hold, a suspension; Charge, Let it go, Dispute, Cancel call, Pay, Pay more, Confirm with your bank, Next round stopping once with its line; Rate stopping once for a stop but never for a pause; a tester's Cancel call refused after a stop; a page opened after it); Stop's required reason (disabled until typed, refused empty or too long, shown to testers in italics and in the email, never in the bell, in Contract changes and the admin timeline, on a paused view too); the tester page after a stop (each gone press from an old page refused and the page moving to the top, the key box gone); a suspended tester's limited view (only Dispute, with the usual stop-once, during either side's suspension; Charge, Let it go and Rate hidden and refused from an old page; excerpt requests hidden until reinstated, then allowed) and their excerpts with Remove on the dashboard; admin search and ticks on Accounts and Tests, the admin scroll boxes (loading past 25, the end line, filters resetting the box, the new-rows pill); Suspend and Reinstate on a profile, with two admins racing, a stale Preview, and suspend against delete; Suspend's layout top to bottom (Preview and its effects full width above the side choice, the reason box and the button, on Accounts, a profile and a phone); Lift the pause (two admins at once, a stale row after a lift naming who and when, a test that closed, a test paused only by a developer hold showing Pause and refusing a Lift, the row turning back into Pause live); the admin test timeline (pay, pause, resume and a suspension in the third person naming who, one line per dispute with the reason in italics, one per decision naming the admin, no "You" or "Your" lines).
- **Cross-device:** a phone action refreshing a desktop tab; a hold reaching every tab; signing in as someone else in another tab; drafts kept after a refused press; sign-in expiring mid-send; network failure during upload or payment; an outage moving timers (a down then an up; an up alone, from its length; a heartbeat gap alone listed with nothing moved, then Approve moving every timer or Dismiss clearing it; an up alert overlapping a gap, applied once with the alert's span and the gap closed; an applied 20-hour outage extended to 26, the extra waiting for Approve; under 15 minutes, nothing; over 24 hours, waiting for Approve; a paused monitor moving nothing); a refused press redrawing the page at once (Join call after a block: the call controls gone; Pay after another tab paid: the Pay box gone; typed text kept); a stop box's times in the saved time zone; the team's Stop read with its time on every path; your own changes in another tab (Pay in tab A stopping Save on Edit in tab B once; an Edit save in A stopping Next round, End early and Request a time in B once, with the line live in B's top box; Rate and excerpt Allow and Deny never stopped by them, the same rating twice refused plainly; the tester's Send in A stopping Charge in B once, a call request in A stopping Dispute in B once but not with Alerts Off, Apply in A stopping Join on B's join page once, Rate in A refusing Rate in B plainly; the same piece paid in both, the typed amount dropped or, for Pay more, kept; an invite in one tab greying the row in the other; the same call request in both; Stop in one tab, then "You stopped the test in another tab" once and a Stop there refused); Edit keeping what you typed (an untouched field taking the other tab's value silently; a typed field keeping its text, with "Saved in another tab or device at …:", their value folded when long, and Use theirs; a later save there updating the note; matching text removing it; Save stopping once per typed field with "…: your version will replace the one saved in another tab at …." and the second press saving yours; the same value: no note and no stop; spots and Open until still taking theirs, with What you typed and Copy; Added and Removed keys under "Changed in another tab or device at …:" and a joining tester's key under "Updated at …:"; a key deleted here but given to a joining tester shown in blue, never red); Payments, profile and Settings changed in another tab (the page updating quietly, the first press stopping once and keeping what was typed); the Alerts chip and menu as one setting (a chip ticking the menu on the same page, after a reload and in another tab; the menu turning the chip back; the tester's "Turn off alerts" and Alerts button); a Pay more in one tab stopping every developer press in another once; never reloading under typing (typing in Request a time while a change arrives: no reload, values kept, the "This page has updates · Refresh" button; an idle page still refreshing); the pill counting every live update while typing (a call request arriving during the Dispute box, an answer or chat).
- **Subscription:** a second test on the free plan (Open, Full and Paused count; drafts, stopped and closed don't), including a draft posted while another test is open; each perk on the free plan (Approved only on Post and Edit, Next round, Invite) refused with its "Subscribers" tag; Cancel, then Keep my subscription before the end; a page open when the subscription ends; running Approved-only tests and sent invites after it ends; a declined renewal (retries, perks during them, the lapse, no hold); a chargeback on a subscription payment; a hold or suspension starting and lifting mid-month (paid days resume, renewal moves later); cancelling during a hold; a sign-up on the 31st; deleting while subscribed; the free-during-launch date (under 30 days refused, the 30- and 7-day notices with email Off, no date set, subscribing during the free period and being charged first on that date).
- **Reviews and payments:** one bell line per payment (an automatic retry of one and of several items naming them, with one email and no receipt line; Pay what you owe's one receipt naming the items; no second line when the bank confirms a waiting charge or the team's Approve lifts a chargeback hold); "confirm by" times for a developer in a time zone far from the server's, near midnight; a dispute on every kind of reviewed part (a $0 Pay, an older Reject, an unlisted Pay), and an admin decision up to the piece's cap, listed or unlisted; Pay more on every paid piece until its cap; a bank confirmation on a press (confirmed, cancelled, the check closed, 15 minutes) and a Charge pressed meanwhile; a charge made while the developer is away waiting for Confirm with your bank, and timing out after 3 days into a hold (or a lapse for a renewal); the dashboard's "Your bank wants you to confirm" (a waiting charge with its deadline and link, a renewal linking to Payments, red under 24 hours, a row leaving live when confirmed elsewhere or expired, shown above the blue box during a hold, hidden when empty); retries during a payment hold (a success lifting it, the 7-try limit, a new card retrying at once); none during a chargeback hold (the daily job, saving a card and verifying a card never charging; a payment hold turning into a chargeback hold stopping its retries; no "next try" line); the $15 fee; a won chargeback before and after paying; Pay what you owe updating live and refused when nothing is owed; paying while suspended.
- **The bell:** the number counting entries, not lines (three lines in one stack count 1, "9+" above 9); expanding a stack or clicking a line reading it, visiting the Notifications page reading nothing; a new line in a read stack counting again; the number following the 20-second check and the tab's focus without navigating; each new stack across two tests on one day (Ratings to give on both sides, Applications and invites, Joined, New terms to answer opening the Switch / Keep box, You were paid with a Pay and a Pay more, Excerpts, Deadlines, the admin's Disputes, Reports and Hold lifts waiting as separate entries, a team pause and a hold in one Payments and holds entry); rating reminders on a running test; the developer's one daily "Pay now" line (one piece, two tests, none once nothing is past its review date) and one bank-confirmation line (two charges the same day in one line, a third the next day in a new line with 3); deadline reminders emailed with email Off, stacked; an admin with email Off getting no summary but the chargeback email at once.
- **Refusals that take the button away:** the kept line where the button was ("Your Join call didn't go through: this call isn't available."), hidden while the button or its own line is still there, gone at the next press; a block dropping Join call, Request a time and the requests from the session page on its poll, both ways.
- **Written work:** the 50,000-character counter; Bold, Italic and Code block rendered safely; 5 attachments of 500 MB (500 MB + 1 byte refused); each answer at least 50 characters (49 refused at that answer, an empty optional question fine, a short draft saved); Record (screen only when the microphone is refused); 5 recordings with Remove, a 6th refused, and Send while one is uploading; long answers in the developer's scroll box; previous question sets (at most 3) and By question grouping; questions not required by default (nothing answered refused with "Answer at least one question before sending.", an empty Required one with "Answer every required question before sending.", the notes box alone not counting, a Required tick changed on Edit offering new terms without moving the current answers to a previous set); every form's mistakes (each red at its field with the same words as the server, a field staying red until its own value is fixed, one linked bullet per red field, the press going to the first one) and the Mature reason (empty, under 5 characters, the counter); End early's and Stop's reasons (0, 4 and 5 characters); the "At most 20 questions." notice (19, 20, then one deleted); a ticked part with a blank price (red, refused) and the Free tick; Edit's greyed Save and the "Not saved yet" / "Not posted yet" badges; the answer form's question box (2, 4 and 20 questions, a long question clamped, a red mistake scrolling to its question); Send with the required question answered (an unlisted written part's default question, a listed part with one required question empty, after a questions change and a Switch); Send my updated answers / recording disabled until something changed (an edit, undoing it, an attachment added or removed, the server refusing an identical resend).
- **Excerpts:** eligible parts only (paid above $0, no NDA); trimming to 10–500 characters; a 4th refused; one pending per part; an NDA added later hiding requests and shown excerpts, and removing it bringing them back; a request pending when an NDA was added, allowed straight into hidden; no "Don't name the product" tick, and older excerpts keeping "a <category> test"; blocks, holds (the dashboard box) and suspensions; deleting on either side; requests never stopping once; the question above each excerpt ("Anything else (bugs, ideas)" for that box), and no excerpts in hover cards; no Take down anywhere for the developer (UI and server: an older page's press refused with "Only the tester can remove an excerpt from their profile."); the tester's Remove sending no bell line.
- **Deletion:** a tester deleting with sent work, with and without a payout account; a developer deleting with work under review, parts open to Charge (charged to the saved card, a decline staying delayed), an open dispute and money owed.
- **Access keys:** the read-only notice updating live as keys are added, deleted or given; a reported key crossed out, with its quiet live-box line; the one Free box (lines deleted and added, another tab's keys left alone, a deleted line whose key a joining tester was given); typing back a key from a tester who left or finished (the warning-only line, never a mistake); the broken-key warning (Select all, Works now, Dismiss selected; a new report showing again; the tester's next press stopping once even with Alerts Off).
- **Sign-in and security:** the one wrong-sign-in message for each case; 5 tries per email and 20 per network; 10 new accounts per network an hour; Forgot password for an account with no password; Google into an unconfirmed account; an unconfirmed admin address; a suspended admin; Sign out of all devices; an upload of the wrong size; the 3 GB a day limit; each reader's own time zone in bell lines and emails; the "I'm 18 or older." tick on email sign-up (unticked, ticked, the server refusing) and on Google's sign-up step (both ticks, an expired step, no account until Continue); an admin account (setup with Name and Pronoun only, landing on /admin, developer and tester pages moving it there, no side switch); emails that fail (Resend failing then recovering, each email sent once; overlapping runs sending nothing twice; 5, 15, 30 minutes then hourly; 48 hours of failures failed for good and kept; Resend's daily limit waiting for the next day without using tries; the admin's red box, the failed list and Clear this list, and "RESEND_API_KEY isn't set on this deploy." on a live deploy without the key; one admin bell per incident, naming the day when it started on an earlier day; /api/health's "degraded: emails" after 15 minutes, still HTTP 200; the dev outbox unchanged without a key).

### Open questions

**Decided by the owner (Oct 28–29)**

- Open until stays one date: it sets the last day to join and the days each tester gets (opened → Open until). No separate "days per tester" field (Rules).
- Close-out: 14 days after the last tester's deadline; each piece keeps at least 14 days of Charge; Pay more closes together with the last Pay / Charge on the test, replacing "30 days after the test ends" (Rules: Close-out; BATCH-17 §19).
- Rate: disabled with its line while something is open; no rating during your own suspension; it stops only for changes about that person; the developer rates only after the tester has rated and everything is settled, and a tester's Rate has no deadline until close-out (Rules: Ratings; BATCH-17 §1, 2, 14, 16).
- A suspended tester's limited view has only Dispute (BATCH-17 §10).
- The Terms of Use's content rule now matches the Mature line ("Never allowed: … Other graphic or mature content must be marked Mature (18+), with a reason."), as Terms version 2, still marked DRAFT for the lawyer (Terms of Use; BATCH-18 §1–2).
- The developer's avatar row reads "+N more joined · M open" (Developer test page; BATCH-18 §4).
- A call ends when its pay would pass what's left of the $1,000 for that tester's calls, and the booked Length is capped by what's left; there's no "Max call time per tester" field (dropped) (Call page; BATCH-18 §3, 6).
- Calls happen in the app's own call room, browser to browser, with no outside video service; "Check your call setup" is an in-app camera and microphone check. The server ends a call for both at 150%, at the $1,000, and at a pause, block, suspension, leave, End early or Stop, with no rejoin, replacing "a call in progress keeps counting until both leave"; the length is the time both browsers were connected; a TURN relay is optional (Call page; BATCH-21, replacing BATCH-18 §9–10, 12–13).
- During a chargeback hold nothing is retried on its own: what is owed is reserved on the card only with Pay what you owe after the card check, and the admin's Approve takes it and lifts the hold in one step; Don't approve releases it, and a reservation lasts about 7 days. Payment holds keep charging at once (Rules: Money; BATCH-22 §1 and BATCH-23 §6, settling BATCH-16 §41; what's added after the reservation: BATCH-28 §8, below).
- A suspended tester is paid normally, like anyone (Rules: Money; BATCH-22 §2).
- Pay that waited for payout setup goes out on its own when the setup is done; the admin list is read-only (Payments; BATCH-22 §3).
- The admin test timeline speaks in the third person, naming who did each thing, with one line per dispute and per decision (Administrator console; BATCH-22 §4).
- Lift the pause shows only on a test the team paused, and a stale or second press is refused naming who lifted it and when (Administrator console; BATCH-22 §5).
- Builder choices confirmed as built: a tester's excerpt request is one bell line per request, stacked in that test's day; after switching to changed questions the tester sends updated answers on the current terms, while answers already sent stay payable at the price they were sent at (BATCH-18 §5, 8).
- No Minutes box on a call's Pay box: it stays Rate + Amount from the measured length, with the Note (BATCH-18 §11, dropped).
- Timed jobs run every 5 minutes from the uptime check, replacing the open infra question; the daily run keeps the daily jobs (Rules: When timed jobs run; BATCH-19 §4).
- The Terms of Use have one "How payments work" section (section 4) and no longer say the developer can lower a call's length, folded into version 2 (Terms of Use; BATCH-19 §3, 6).
- Post a test and Edit have a required Charge tick, and the Open until line says that work can arrive after Open until; there's no extra line under the tester's Charge box (Post a test, Edit test; BATCH-19 §7–8).
- Age is confirmed only at sign-up (email and a Google step), with "You must be 18 or older to use Playtest." in the Terms; existing accounts aren't asked, and the updated-Terms tick has no age part (Every page: Sign-in and accounts; Terms of Use; BATCH-20 §5, 21).
- An admin account is only an admin: no developer or tester side, ever (Administrator console; BATCH-20 §16).
- A tester's rating never stops a developer press and isn't an Alerts type; only a working tester's leave (by rating) stops, under Testers leaving (Why a press stops; BATCH-20 §24).
- A profile you blocked still opens, with Unblock; one whose owner blocked you reads "This profile isn't available." (Public profiles; BATCH-20 §17).
- Read-only question lists and past calls scroll past 3, with no "+N more" (Long lists on Every page; BATCH-20 §1, 11).
- Emails never get lost: stored first, retried for 48 hours, then kept as failed for good; the owner hears through the admin's red box, one bell line per incident, and the uptime monitor's "degraded: emails" keyword (Every page: Emails; Security and launch; BATCH-23 §1–2).
- Edit keeps what you typed and shows what the other tab saved, with Use theirs and one stop line per typed field on Save; spots, Open until and access keys keep their rules; key notes say who changed them ("Changed in another tab or device at" for your own saves, "Updated at" for other people's). This replaces BATCH-16 §19's "the box takes the other tab's value" (Edit test; BATCH-23 §5).
- Suspend keeps its Preview, laid out above the side choice, the reason box and the button (Administrator console; BATCH-23 §7, replacing "remove Preview").
- Confirm with your bank stops once exactly like Pay on that piece: the same check, the same lock and the same Alerts types, so whatever stops Pay on it stops Confirm (Developer test page; BATCH-23 §9, BATCH-26 §9).
- Every charge waiting for the developer's bank is listed at the very top of the developer dashboard, "Your bank wants you to confirm (N)", above the blue box during a hold or suspension too and hidden when empty: soonest deadline first in a scroll box about 3 rows tall, red under 24 hours, each with Confirm with your bank → to its piece on the test page (a renewal: its row on Payments), live (Developer dashboard; BATCH-24 §1).
- A tester who rates, finishing their part, closes their waiting call requests with "the tester finished their part"; Leave and a tester-side suspension keep "the tester left" (Tester session page; BATCH-24 §2).
- The team's Approve that lifts a chargeback hold sends the developer one notice naming both, "The team approved lifting your hold: the $X reserved on your card was taken, and your tests are running again.", instead of two (Administrator console; BATCH-24, a BATCH-23 leftover).
- Post keeps its Open until hint and line as they are, and Edit keeps its live box and its "N new updates" pill, whose arrow points down when the box under Save is below the screen (BATCH-23 §4 and §8, dropped; BATCH-27 §4).
- The bank's "confirm by" times (the dashboard's rows, Payments' list, the test page's "Confirm it by" and a call's time there) are in the developer's own saved time zone: the time, "today" / "tomorrow" and the red under-24-hours check (Developer dashboard; BATCH-25 §1).
- A reversed payment charged again reads the same everywhere: "a payment your bank had reversed (Ana Silva's written answers)"; an automatic retry of what's owed keeps "what you owe" (Developer dashboard; BATCH-25 §2).
- The renewal's bell line opens its own row on Payments, highlighted (Payments; BATCH-25 §3).
- One bell line for the same money: an automatic retry gives one line in Payments and holds naming what was paid ("We charged your card $10.00 for what you owed: Ana Silva's call on Oct 30."), with the payment email as its receipt and no receipt line; Pay what you owe gives one receipt naming the items; the bank's confirmation and the team's Approve never add a second line (Every page: The bell; BATCH-25 §5).
- A real outage is always recorded: an up alert with no down records it from the length it carries; reports of the same downtime merge into one outage and one bell line; the usual 15-minute and 24-hour rules hold (Every page: Outages and phones; BATCH-26 §1). The heartbeat part is replaced by BATCH-28 §1–2 (Built with defaults, below): a heartbeat gap never moves anything on its own.
- A block ends a call in progress between the two at once (Call page; BATCH-26 §2).
- Testers never read who stopped a test the team stopped: "This test was stopped at 3:10 PM." everywhere (stop boxes, banners, refusals, the live box); every stop line says its time once (Stopped tests; BATCH-26 §3–4).
- The developer's chargeback box says "chargeback hold" (Payments; BATCH-26 §5).
- Invite follows the test live both ways, with its own check, not the page's refresh (Developer test page; BATCH-26 §7).
- A tester who rated reads "finished" in Worked with you, never "left" (Developer test page; BATCH-26 §8).
- Call request rows say live when a request closed or was answered elsewhere, and a refusal names the press ("Your Withdraw didn't go through: …") (Developer test page; BATCH-26 §10).
- Sign-up marks every missing tick red at once, each line under its whole tick row, on both the email form and the Google step; the Required question's "*" sits right after its words (Every page: Sign-in and accounts; Tester session page; BATCH-26 §11).
- The admin's Payouts waiting is the plain admin scroll box, every one, with "That's all (N)" (Administrator console; BATCH-26 §12), oldest first, because the longest-waiting are the most urgent (BATCH-28 §7).
- The device pop-up closes after Apply anyway or Ask to rejoin anyway goes through; Join anyway moves on to the session page (Join page; BATCH-26 §13).
- Questions are not required by default: the developer ticks Required on any a tester must answer, and testers answer at least one question to send; questions stored before this (required unless marked "not required") all became not required (Post a test, Tester session page; BATCH-26 §14).
- Every answer set reads the same: the question as a small grey label above its indented answer, "(no answer)" in grey for an empty one (Tester session page; BATCH-26 §15).
- An open offer of new terms blocks the tester's Send (answers and recording) and every call press, Join call included, until they press Switch or Keep: "Answer the new terms first: Switch or Keep." under the button, the page moves up to the highlighted offer, and the server refuses the same way, old pages too; Keep unlocks like Switch, Alerts Off doesn't lift it, and Charge, Let it go, Dispute, Rate and Leave keep their stop-once. This replaces the one-time stop "Press again to send under your current terms, or switch first." for those presses (Why a press stops: New terms come first; BATCH-27 §1).
- "Go to the next round" is a press that stops once like Next round, and opens the draft on the second press (on a stopped, closed or paused test with the same stop-once since BATCH-29 §11) (Developer test page; BATCH-27 §2).
- The live box never scrolls the page on its own: every update while it's out of view raises the pill, whose arrow points up or down to the box, typing or not. This replaces BATCH-15 §28's first-update scroll (Every page; BATCH-27 §4).
- Admin Tests rows follow a pause set or lifted elsewhere at once (on focus, a visible tab and the live update, searched or not), and a lifted pause reads "open · paused by <email>, lifted by <email> at <time>" (Administrator console; BATCH-27 §5).
- Outside production a call may be requested 1 minute ahead (CALL_MIN_AHEAD_MINUTES), so calls can be tested locally; the live site keeps 5 minutes (Developer test page; BATCH-27 §6).
- A card saved through Link works like any card: Add a card and Change card save only cards and Link, and the developer's presses accept both (Payments).
- During a chargeback hold, Pay what you owe reserves once: a tester's Charge (or anything else) added after the reservation never blocks the team's Approve, which takes the reservation and lifts the hold as usual; what was added is charged right after the lift on the verified card, as an ordinary charge with its own receipt (the bank asking: the confirm box; declined: a normal payment hold). This replaces BATCH-23 §6's "Approve is refused while short" and its second reservation for the difference (Payments, Rules: Money; BATCH-28 §8).
- A press refused because the state changed redraws the page at once, on every page and both sides, without a full reload and keeping typed text (Every page: How state stays consistent; BATCH-28 §9).
- A dispute can raise a piece's pay up to the piece's own cap (the same cap Pay and Pay more have), not just the listing, because the two may have agreed more in chat; Dispute is open while the piece is below its cap. To decide, the team can read the tester's and developer's chat on that test in the dispute row's "Their chat on this test" fold, the only place admins can read a chat, with no read log; the Terms say so (Administrator console, Rules: Dispute range; BATCH-28 §15).
- A listed piece's cap is its listing plus the part's max ($1,000 for written answers or a recording; $200/h × the measured length for a call, within the $1,000 for that tester's calls), replacing the limit of 100% of the listing on extra pay (BATCH-28 §20); corrected by BATCH-29 §38 below: the part's max in all, not on top of the listing.
- Listed calls are charged automatically 7 days after they take place if the developer hasn't paid them, for the measured length × the contract rate; there's no Charge or Let it go for listed calls, and the Charge tick and the Terms say so. Unlisted calls are unchanged (Rules: Money; BATCH-28 §21).
- Payment lines are emailed the same evening whatever the email setting, Off included (Every page: Emails; BATCH-28 §18).
- The developer's daily line after a part's review date names that date and says until when they can still pay: "“<test>”: <tester>'s <part> wasn't reviewed by <date>: <tester> can now charge or let it go. You can still pay until they do (at the latest <date>)." (Oct 29); replaced by the one daily "Pay now" line across tests (BATCH-29 §31, below), except for a piece the tester can't Charge, which keeps "…wasn't reviewed by <date>: pay it."
- Stopped, closed and paused tests (any cause), a tester's work that ended (ended early, left, removed, blocked) and a held or suspended developer keep the same stop-once rules as a running test: the first press after a change the page hasn't shown stops once with the Alerts lines, the second goes ahead. The stop, close or pause itself is a change, so a page opened before it stops once naming it ("Nora Lane stopped this test at 3:10 PM.", "This test was paused at 3:10 PM.", "The team paused this test at 3:10 PM."). Rate keeps its own short list (a pause never stops it), excerpt Allow, Deny and Remove and "Send your side" keep their rules, Leave and Block never stop, and refusals stay plain. This replaces BATCH-16 §25's "goes through on the first press" (Stopped tests on Every page; BATCH-29 §11).
- The developer's own changes in another tab or device (an Edit save, Pay, Pay more, Confirm with your bank, End early, Stop, Next round, any call change, Invite or Cancel invite, Accept or Decline) stop every developer press once on the test page and Edit, as "You … in another tab" lines ("You saved the listing in another tab: spots, description", "You started the next round in another tab"), shown live in the top box; they aren't in Alerts. Edit keeps its per-field notes as well. This widens BATCH-16 §21 and the Oct 28 "big changes" rule (Why a press stops; BATCH-29 §12).
- The tester's own changes in another tab or device work the same way on the session page, the join page and their invitations ("You sent your updated answers in another tab", "You applied to join in another tab"), following Alerts (Off: they don't stop); Rate and excerpt requests and Remove only race with the same press ("You already rated Nora Lane."), and a Leave made elsewhere is a line that stops the others (Why a press stops; BATCH-29 §13).
- A refused press whose button the redraw takes away keeps its line where the button was ("Your Join call didn't go through: this call isn't available.") until the next press; a block drops the session page's call controls on its normal poll (Every page: How state stays consistent; BATCH-29 §36).
- Leftovers of the batch 27 check: a dashboard Join call refused for new terms links "Answer them on the session page →" to the highlighted Switch / Keep box; Charge and Let it go end "Press again to go ahead under your current terms, or switch first."; the admin Tests row says "the team" when an admin's name is missing, "at" before the time, pairs a lift with the pause it ended, and reads "closed" (no Lift the pause) for a team-paused test closed after 7 days; the live check covers rows loaded beyond the first 25; "Pick a time in the future." everywhere; the server passes its call lead to the forms (BATCH-29 §20).
- Rating reminders come on running tests too, on both sides, and every rating line stacks in one "Ratings to give" entry a day across tests, opening the dashboard's box (Rules: Ratings; BATCH-29 §21).
- The developer's closing-soon reminders follow the countdown (1 month, 1 week, 3 days, 1 day), one line a day across tests, kept alongside "Pay now"; the tester's Charge offer, its reminders and their close-out lines become one daily "Charge or let go" line across tests (Rules: Close-out; BATCH-29 §22).
- The tester's applications and invites, joins, new terms to answer, payments (Pay more included), excerpt answers and deadline reminders each stack across tests, one entry per kind per day (Every page: The bell; BATCH-29 §23–27).
- The bell's number counts entries with anything unread, not lines ("9+" above 9); a line is read when clicked and a stack when expanded, never by visiting the page; the number is live on every page (Every page: The bell; BATCH-29 §28).
- The admin's disputes, reports and hold lifts waiting each get their own daily entry across tests, each line opening its row (Administrator console; BATCH-29 §29).
- Only testers remove excerpts: the developer's Take down is gone (an older page's press reads "Only the tester can remove an excerpt from their profile."), and a tester's Remove sends no bell line (Shared pages; BATCH-29 §30).
- The developer gets one "Pay now" line a day across all their tests while any listed piece is past its review date and open to the tester's Charge (Rules: Test lifecycle; BATCH-29 §31).
- One bank-confirmation line covers every charge waiting for the bank, across tests and renewals, at most one a day, opening the dashboard's box (Every page: The bell; BATCH-29 §32).
- The team's pause, stop and close lines stack with the hold lines in "Payments and holds" (Every page: The bell; BATCH-29 §33).
- The admin summary follows each admin's own email setting; "Playtest: new chargeback of $X" still goes at once to every admin; every tester deadline reminder is emailed whatever the setting, stacked (Every page: Emails; BATCH-29 §34).
- A listed call's automatic charge that waits (a hold, a suspension, the bank) or is declined reads "Payment for this call may be delayed." for the tester, with the "may be delayed" bell line; the developer's page records the true outcome; a deleted developer's unpaid listed calls are charged on their saved card (no card: can't be paid); the automatic-charge dates use each reader's saved zone (Rules: Money; BATCH-29 §37).
- A piece's cap is the part's max in all, listed or not, Pay, Pay more and dispute raises together: $1,000 for written answers or a recording; $200/h × the measured minutes for a call, within the $1,000 for that tester's calls. One refusal line: "This is more than this piece can be paid (up to $990 more, $1,000 in all)." This replaces "listing + max" and the unlisted "$2,000 in all" (Rules: Extra pay cap; BATCH-29 §38).
- The Block pop-up says what a block does on each side: sessions end (sent work stays payable), calls are cancelled, applications and invites close, chat closes, and who no longer sees whose tests (Public profiles and hover cards).
- Verify a card's form shows cards only: no Link bank tab and no wallets, since the check needs a card with 3D Secure (Payments).
- A paid part can be sent again; below the cap the update gets Pay more in Needs you, at the cap only a quiet line (Rules: Money; BATCH-30 §2).
- Listed work sent after the tester's own deadline never opens Charge or Let it go; the Charge tick and the Terms say Charge applies to work sent by the deadline (Rules: Test lifecycle; BATCH-30 §3).
- The team can close a developer's or a test's open reviews at $0 with a reason, and it's final: no dispute on a team closure (Administrator console; BATCH-30 §5).
- Invite search shows why a matching person can't be invited; new invites and an application's answer show live; a locked field's change is named with Put it back; testers read one line per listing save, and stops never swallow a change (BATCH-30 §7–§12).
- Each written answer is at least 50 characters; the test's name and steps at least 10 (BATCH-30 §14, §18).
- The session page shows the current listing information inside "Your agreed scope", next to the fixed contract; a Save gives working testers without a key a free key (BATCH-30 §15, §17).
- Emails are branded HTML with the plain text kept; a piece's review date reads the same day on both sides (BATCH-30 §19–§20).
- Every test takes at most 10 testers (the growing limit and the profile's tester-limit card are gone; a test with more keeps them); files on written answers can be 500 MB each (BATCH-31).
- Coming later, not built yet: removing the developer's Rate stop for call changes, which can never apply (BATCH-30 §4); the leftovers of the batch 29 doc check (BATCH-30 §6); storage alerts for the admins (BATCH-30 §35).

**Decided, no change (Oct 29)**

- After a stop, End early, Leave, a block or a removal, a sent piece's review date stays the later of 7 days after sending and the tester's original deadline: Charge doesn't open 7 days after the work ended (Rules: Test lifecycle; BATCH-29).
- Charge / Let it go stays for listed written answers and recordings: listed work isn't charged automatically at its review date; only listed calls are (Rules: Money; BATCH-30).
- The tester's Rate still closes at the test's close-out, not at their own pieces' closing date (Rules: Ratings; BATCH-30).
- Late sending stays: a tester can still send after their own deadline ("Sent late (deadline was <date>)"), and work isn't closed at the deadline; only Charge changed for late work (Rules: Test lifecycle; BATCH-30).

**Waiting for the owner (tonight's list)**

- Built with defaults, to confirm:
  - A block's call end line: the person who blocked reads "Call ended: you blocked <name>."; the other reads "Call ended: the call was cancelled.", never that they were blocked (BATCH-26 §2).
  - A sent, listed part tells the tester when Charge opens, on their dashboard and under the part on the session page: "If <dev> hasn't paid by <date>, you can charge or let it go then." (BATCH-26 §6, BATCH-28 §5). A listed call says instead "If <dev> doesn't pay by <date>, it's charged automatically." (BATCH-28 §21).
  - A heartbeat gap is never applied on its own: it's listed in the admin's Outages box as "Possible outage: no checks from 3:10 PM to 3:42 PM (32 min). Nothing has moved." with Approve: move every timer and Dismiss, at any length; any server activity counts as a heartbeat; a reported outage's span wins and closes a gap it covers; an extension that brings an applied outage over 24 hours waits for Approve (Every page: Outages and phones; BATCH-28 §1–2).
  - When a piece can't be paid more, both sides say so: the developer reads "Paid the most this piece can be paid ($X)." where Pay more was; the tester's paid part reads "<dev> has paid $X for this. It can be paid more, up to $Y in all.", "This part is already paid the most it can be ($X).", or "Pay more closed on <date>." (the wording is a default; BATCH-28 §14).
  - A tester on older call terms than the listing shows on the developer's request row as "Their terms: $30/h (the listing is now $24/h)" (BATCH-26 §10).
  - While a test is paused, testers can still write and save drafts.
  - When a pause lifts, every working tester's deadline and the test's Open until move later by the length of the pause.
  - A deleted tester with no payout account: their sent work leaves Needs you with "<name> deleted their account before setting up payouts, so this can't be paid." (BATCH-11 §1).
  - Quiet Switch / Keep: a Keep tells the developer nothing, and a Switch is one quiet line that never stops a press (BATCH-14 §4).
  - The review date (Charge, reminders, rating) uses each tester's own deadline in place of Open until (BATCH-15 §18).
  - A test whose joining has closed no longer counts toward the open tests (BATCH-15 §18).
  - A payment or chargeback hold gives the same paused view as a suspension: Stop, End early and Cancel call (BATCH-15 §29).
  - A refused press on a test the team paused goes to the test page's banner, not the dashboard top (BATCH-15 §23).
  - A tester who finished counts as "no longer working", so their key can be typed back into Free (BATCH-15 §19).
  - The developer marking a tester's key as working stops that tester's next press even with Alerts Off (BATCH-15 §45).
  - Pay what you owe stops once for a changed amount in every state, a hold included, because money would act on something unseen (BATCH-16 §25). (Every other press keeps the usual stop-once in those states since BATCH-29 §11; Rate's own stops are settled: BATCH-17 §14.)
  - Rate and excerpt Allow and Deny are never stopped by the developer's own changes in another tab, and ratings and excerpt decisions made there aren't lines that stop other presses (the default reading of the owner's words, BATCH-29 §12).
  - The tester's daily line names their own work as "your": "Charge or let go: your written answers on “Harbor Run” ($10.00, by Nov 18)" (BATCH-29 §22's example named the tester).
  - A stop of any kind (the developer's Stop, the team's Stop, a close, End early for that tester) is treated alike when a page is open (BATCH-17 §12).
  - Close-out protections: the "closes on" lines in the last 7 days, the reminders 3 days and 1 day before (emailed whatever the setting), and a suspended tester's time added to their Charge windows (BATCH-17 §19).
  - After close-out, a developer can rate a never-rated tester only if that tester sent some work or had a call (BATCH-17 §19).
  - Leave is hidden after a stop, a close or an early end: the tester's work is over, and their part ends when they rate (BATCH-16 §24).
- Files placed at the cursor in written answers (BATCH-27 §3, bringing back BATCH-16 §34 before launch): not built, because the owner hasn't chosen its design. Today files and recordings go in the "Files and recordings" box under the answers.
- Remove "Listing changes" from the developer's Alerts menu? The owner is deciding; today it's still a type there (Developer test page: Alerts).
- Payout setup is not required before joining or working. Pay waits for setup and then goes out on its own; reminders total the waiting shares (Payments; BATCH-32 §1).
- Confirm large totals before posting ("Up to $8,000 could be charged if all spots send everything.")? Not built.
- When a limit in Rules changes, tell developers in the bell and on the fields it affects?
- Fraud: invite developers by hand at launch, a longer tester payout delay, and a new-developer spending limit or prepaid budgets before public developer sign-up?
- Pause developers who stop responding? Not built.
- 3D Secure on large payments (e.g. Pay over $200): not built; revisit after launch data.
- Tests paused repeatedly, and repeated holds or suspensions: stop a test or escalate after several? (The old "third pause stops the test" idea was dropped.)
- Tester suspensions: removing a suspended tester from all tests at once may be too harsh. Options: pause their contracts instead, or let developers choose.
- UI for many rows and long text: long names and titles are cut with "…" (full text on hover); reasons and notes sit in scroll boxes. Revisit layouts for accounts with many rows and very long names.
- Written answers: are 50,000 characters per written part, and 5 attachments of 500 MB each, the right limits?
- Which formats do developers want in written answers? Today: Bold, Italic and Code block.

**Earlier questions**

- **Payments:** is a 10-day refund period workable with the payment provider's rules and payout timing; refund exceptions; pay when a developer ends work early.
- **Restrictions:** different escalation for different causes; stopping abusive re-registration without blocking people who share devices or networks; reports on paused tests.
- **Business:** higher prices for higher-rated testers; charging for work that isn't accepted; which tester stats are public; forums or group tests.
- **Ratings:** scales and categories; early departures; tests ending without full completion.
- **Content and storage:** which content needs warnings or rejection; identity checks; how long recordings are kept and size limits; resumable uploads; transcription with self-hosted open-source models; phone layouts for complex panels; taking back a dispute; more than 10 tests per tester.

### Post-launch

Files placed at the cursor in written answers (an attach and a record button in each answer's toolbar, the files shown in place; for launch the "Files and recordings" box under the written answers stays: BATCH-16 §34); tester notification preferences; admin outage recording; admin pay above the listing; notification muting; mute chat (quiet a person without blocking); more rating filters; question-based forums; group tests; advanced recruitment; automated risk controls; Themes and moments (transcribed recordings grouped into themes); an admin "Payout problems" list; a spending limit or prepaid budgets; in-app messages between admins and users; a soft "Ana is looking at this" marker on disputes; link checks on the build link (block private network addresses, check for malware and phishing with Google Web Risk, warn about password-protected archives); if invalid or unreachable emails are found, require those accounts to change their email (and password) before continuing.

### Launch priorities

1. Core marketplace: posting, recruiting, joining, sending work, history.
2. Payments: charges, payouts, declines, balances, disputes.
3. Lifecycle: open, paused, closed, deadlines, late joins.
4. Contract integrity: stable terms, correct prices, no conflicting updates.
5. Restrictions: holds, chargebacks, suspensions, deletion, reinstatement.
6. Consistency: cross-tab updates, old forms, repeated presses, failure notices.
7. Reputation: ratings and history.
8. Admin tools: records, reports, suspensions, disputes.
9. Phones and recording reliability.
10. Post-launch items.
